88 XtalPi Holdings Limited Annual Report 2025
董事報告
Report of Directors
(g) 歸屬期
任何獎勵的歸屬期不得少於12個月。如在以下
情況下，獎勵的歸屬期可能較短：
(1) 在所有情況下，倘授出購股權旨在承擔或
替代或交換先前由本公司或任何附屬公司收購
的公司或本公司或任何附屬公司與之合併的公
司授出的獎勵或日後授出獎勵的權利或義務；
(2) 歸屬有關獎勵而發行股份前須持有購股權
的最短期限為12個月且股份乃根據合資格參與
者與本公司之薪酬安排而交付該人士，包括根
據有關非僱員董事之年度留任酬金交付於該非
僱員董事的股份；
(3) 獎勵為對新僱員參與者的簽約或全額授
予；
(4) 獎勵須達成績效掛鉤歸屬條件；
(5) 因行政或合規原因分批授出獎勵；
(6) 獎勵應於12個月或以上期間均等歸屬；
(7) 獎勵的歸屬及持有期合共超過12個月；或
(8) 在退任、離職、留任安排、身故、殘疾或
本公司控制權變動的情況下，董事會可全權酌
情加速獎勵歸屬。
(h) 首次公開發售後受限制股份單位計劃的期
限
在若干終止事項的規限下，首次公開發售後受
限制股份單位計劃自上市日期起五(5)年（「首次
公開發售後受限制股份單位計劃期」）內有效，
期滿後不再授出獎勵，但首次公開發售後受限
制股份單位計劃條文在所有其他方面仍具有十
足效力及作用，惟須以有效行使該計劃前所授
出獎勵或在首次公開發售後受限制股份單位計
劃條文規定的其他情況下有效行使為限，而於
首次公開發售後受限制股份單位計劃期內授出
的獎勵可繼續根據首次公開發售後受限制股份
單位計劃行使。
(g) Vesting period
The vesting period for any awards shall not be less than 12 months. 
Awards may be subject to a shorter vesting period under any of the 
following circumstances:
(1) where the awards are granted in assumption of, or in substitution or 
exchange for, an award previously granted, or the right or obligation to 
make a future award, in all cases by a company acquired by our Group or 
with which our Group combines;
(2) where the Shares to be issued upon the vesting of such awards 
are subject to a minimum holding period of not less than 12 months 
and are delivered to an eligible participant under his/her compensation 
arrangements with our Company, including Shares delivered to a non￾employee Director in respect of such non-employee Director’s annual 
retainer;
(3) where the awards are sign-on or make-whole grants to new eligible 
participants;
(4) where the awards are subject to performance-based vesting 
conditions;
(5) where the awards are granted in batches for administrative or 
compliance reasons;
(6) where the awards shall vest evenly over a period of 12 months or 
more;
(7) where the awards are subject to a total vesting and holding period of 
more than 12 months; or
(8) in cases of retirement, separation, retention arrangements, death, 
disability or a change in control of our Company, our Board may accelerate 
the vesting of the awards at its sole discretion.
(h) Duration of the Post-IPO RSU Scheme
Subject to certain termination events, the Post-IPO RSU Scheme shall be 
valid and effective for the period of five (5) years commencing on the 
Listing Date (the “Post-IPO RSU Scheme Period”), after which period 
no further awards will be granted, but the provisions of the Post-IPO RSU 
Scheme shall in all other respects remain in full force and effect to the 
extent necessary to give effect to the awards granted prior thereto or 
otherwise as may be required in accordance with the provisions of the 
Post-IPO RSU Scheme and the awards that are granted during the Post-IPO 
RSU Scheme Period shall continue to be exercisable in accordance with the 
Post-IPO RSU Scheme.

(the “Guarantors”) and Shanghai Siwei Medical Technology Co., Ltd.* 
(上海四維醫學科技有限公司) (the “Target Company”) entered into 
the agreement (the “Agreement”), pursuant to which the Purchaser 
conditionally agreed to purchase, and the Vendors conditionally agreed 
to sell the 90% equity interest in the Target Company at a consideration 
of RMB250 million, to be fully settled by cash. Upon completion, the 
Purchaser was interested in 90% equity interest in the Target Company. 
Each member of the Target Group will become an indirect non-wholly 
owned subsidiary of the Company.

Report of Directors
賣方及擔保人向買方承諾，目標公司於截至
2025年12月31日止財政年度（「表現保證年
度」）來自上海地區項目的主營業務收入不得少
於人民幣27百萬元（「保證收入」）。
於表現保證年度結束後90個營業日內，買方與
賣方須共同委聘一家合資格的第三方會計師事
務所，就目標公司於表現保證年度的實際實現
收入（「實際實現收入」）進行獨立審計。
若實際實現收入低於保證收入，買方有權要求
賣方及擔保人以現金補償買方該保證收入與實
際實現收入之間的差額。
截至2025年12月31日止財政年度，該保證收
入已獲達成。
上市所得款項用途
就本公司的全球發售而言，於上市日期已
按每股5.28港元發行187,373,000股每股面
值0.00001美元的普通股。於2024年7月10
日，我們根據超額配股權的部分行使情況按
每股5.28港元額外發行8,796,000股每股面值
0.00001美元的普通股。
The Vendors and the Guarantors undertook to the Purchaser that the 
Target Company’s revenue from its principal business generated from 
projects in Shanghai region for the financial year ending 31 December 
2025 (the “Performance Guarantee Year”) shall not be less than 
RMB27 million (the “Guaranteed Revenue”).
Within 90 business days after the end of the Performance Guaranteed 
Year, the Purchaser and the Vendors shall jointly engage a third party 
qualified accounting firm to conduct an independent audit of the Target 
Company with respect to the actual realised revenue for the Performance 
Guarantee Year (the “Actual Realised Revenue”).
In the event that the Actual Realised Revenue is lower than the 
Guaranteed Revenue, the Purchaser shall have the right to request the 
Vendors and the Guarantors to compensate the Purchaser the shortfall 
between the Guaranteed Revenue and the Actual Realised Revenue by 
cash.
During the financial year ended 31 December 2025, such Guaranteed 
Revenue has been met.

USE OF PROCEEDS FROM THE PLACINGS
January Placing
On 19 January 2025, the Company announced the placing of 264,000,000 
new Shares to not less than six placees at the placing price of HK$4.28 
per Share (the “January Placing”), which was completed on 24 January 
2025. To the best of the Directors’ knowledge, information and belief, 
having made all reasonable enquiries, the placees were professional, 
institutional, or other investors that are, together with their respective 
ultimate beneficial owners, third parties independent of the Company and 
its connected persons.
The aggregate nominal value of the Shares under the January Placing 
is US$2,640. The market price of the Shares as quoted on the Stock 
Exchange on 17 January 2025, being the last trading date immediately 
prior to the date on which the terms of the January Placing were fixed, 
was HK$4.65 per Share.
The gross proceeds from the January Placing amounted to approximately 
HK$1,130.00 million, and the net proceeds (after deducting all applicable 
costs and expenses, including commission and levies) arising from the 
January Placing amounted to approximately HK$1,125.00 million. On this 
basis, the net price per Share under the January Placing was approximately 
HK$4.26.
The Company intends to use the net proceeds from the January Placing 
primarily for (i) continuous product iterations and upgrades to enhance 
its research and development technological capabilities and solution 
capabilities, (ii) facilitating the commercialization of the Company, 
strengthening external collaborations, and expanding the scale and market 
share of the Company, and (iii) investing in potential opportunities, talent 
attraction and introduction, working capital replenishment and general 
corporate purposes.
To the extent that the net proceeds from the January Placing are not 
immediately used for the purposes described above, the Company may 
hold such proceeds in short-term deposits or purchase short-term wealth 
management products so long as it is deemed to be in the best interests 
of the Company.
截至2025年12月31日，董事並不知悉所得款
項淨額擬定用途的任何重大變動。所有未動用
的所得款項淨額已存入本集團在持牌商業銀
行及╱或其他授權金融機構開立的短期計息賬
戶。
配售所得款項用途
一月配售
於2025年1月19日，本公司宣佈以每股4.28
港元的配售價，向不少於六名承配人配售
264,000,000股新股（「一月配售」），本次配售
已於2025年1月24日完成。據董事作出一切合
理查詢後所深知、盡悉及確信，各承配人均為
專業投資者、機構投資者或其他投資者，連同
彼等各自最終實益擁有人均為獨立於本公司及
其關連人士的第三方。
一月配售項下股份的總面值為2,640美元。聯
交所於2025年1月17日（即緊接釐定一月配售
條款當日前的最後一個交易日）所報之股份市
價為每股4.65港元。
一月配售所得款項總額約為1,130.00百萬
港元，經扣除所有相關成本及開支（包括佣
金及徵費）後，一月配售所得款項淨額約為
1,125.00百萬港元。據此計算，一月配售項下
每股股份的淨價格約為4.26港元。
本公司擬動用一月配售所得款項淨額，主要用
於(i)產品持續迭代升級，提升研發技術能力和
解決方案能力；(ii)促進本公司的商業化發展，
加強外部合作，擴大本公司規模和市場份額；
及(iii)潛在機會投資、人才吸引與引進、營運
資金補充和一般公司用途。
倘一月配售所得款項淨額並未立即用於上述用
途，只要被視作符合本公司的最佳利益，本公
司可將該等所得款項持作短期存款或購買短期
理財產品。

二月配售
於2025年2月19日，本公司宣佈以每股6.10
港元的配售價，向不少於六名承配人配售
342,288,000股新股（「二月配售」），本次配售
已於2025年2月25日完成。據董事作出一切合
理查詢後所深知、盡悉及確信，各承配人均為
專業投資者、機構投資者或其他投資者，連同
彼等各自最終實益擁有人均為獨立於本公司及
其關連人士的第三方。
二月配售項下股份的總面值為3,422.88美元。
聯交所於2025年2月18日（即釐定二月配售條
款當日）所報之股份市價為每股6.48港元。
二月配售所得款項總額約為2,088.00百萬
港元，經扣除所有相關成本及開支（包括佣
金及徵費）後，二月配售所得款項淨額約為
2,080.00百萬港元。據此計算，二月配售項下
每股股份的淨價格約為6.08港元。
本公司擬動用二月配售所得款項淨額，主要用
於(i)產品持續迭代升級，提升研發技術能力和
解決方案能力，數據生成及模型建立；(ii)產品
商業化及業務發展，加強與從事於與本集團類
似及互惠互利行業的外部各方及合作夥伴的
業務合作，擴大本公司的規模及市場份額；及
(iii)潛在機會收購及投資、人才吸引與引進、
營運資金補充和一般公司用途。
倘二月配售所得款項淨額並未立即用於上述用
途，只要被視作符合本公司及其股東的最佳利
益，本公司可將該等所得款項持作短期存款或
購買短期理財產品。
February Placing
On 19 February 2025, the Company announced the placing of 
342,288,000 new Shares to not less than six placees at the placing price 
of HK$6.10 per Share (the “February Placing”), which was completed on 
25 February 2025. To the best of the Directors’ knowledge, information 
and belief, having made all reasonable enquiries, the placees were 
professional, institutional, or other investors that are, together with their 
respective ultimate beneficial owners, third parties independent of the 
Company and its connected persons.
The aggregate nominal value of the Shares under the February Placing 
is US$3,422.88. The market price of the Shares as quoted on the Stock 
Exchange on 18 February 2025, being the date on which the terms of the 
February Placing were fixed, was HK$6.48 per Share.
The gross proceeds from the February Placing amounted to approximately 
HK$2,088.00 million, and the net proceeds (after deducting all applicable 
costs and expenses, including commission and levies) arising from the 
February Placing amounted to approximately HK$2,080.00 million. 
On this basis, the net price per Share under the February Placing was 
approximately HK$6.08.
The Company intends to use the net proceeds from the February Placing 
primarily for (i) continuous product iterations and upgrades to enhance 
its research and development technological capabilities and solution 
capabilities, and data generation and model building; (ii) product 
commercialisation and business development, strengthening business 
collaborations with external parties and partners which are engaged in 
similar and mutually beneficial industries as the Group, and expanding the 
scale and market share of the Company; and (iii) acquisitions and investing 
in potential opportunities, talent attraction and introduction, working 
capital replenishment and general corporate purposes.
To the extent that the net proceeds from the February Placing are not 
immediately used for the purposes described above, the Company may 
hold such proceeds in short-term deposits or purchase short-term wealth 
management products so long as it is deemed to be in the best interests 
of the Company and its shareholders.

期後事項
於2026年1月28日，本公司完成發行本金總
額2,866,000,000港元的零息可轉換債券（「債
券」）。發行債券所得款項淨額於扣除相關費
用、佣金及其他估計開支後估計約為2,836.6
百萬港元。
有關進一步詳情，請參閱本公司日期為2026
年1月29日的公告。
自2025年12月31日以後及直至本報告日期，
本集團已與多間實體簽訂多個股權相關投資並
認購多個理財產品，總對價分別約為人民幣
423.3百萬元及人民幣412.8百萬元。
股東週年大會及暫停辦理股份過戶
登記
本公司將根據上市規則儘快安排召開股東週年
大會的時間。召開股東週年大會的通告將按照
上市規則及組織章程細則規定的方式適時刊發
並寄發予股東。待股東週年大會日期確定後，
本公司將於股東週年大會通告中公佈本公司暫
停辦理股份過戶登記期間。

於本年報第42至62頁企業管治報告。

根據公開可得的資料及據董事所知，截至本年
報日期，本公司已按照上市規則的要求維持規
定公眾持股量（即25%）。
As of 31 December 2025, the Directors were not aware of any material 
change in the planned use of the net proceeds. To the extent that the 
net proceeds from the January Placing, February Placing and September

Company may hold such proceeds in short-term deposits or purchase 
short-term wealth management products so long as it is deemed to be in 
the best interests of the Company.

Environmental, Social and Governance Report
ABOUT THE REPORT
This Environmental, Social and Governance Report (the “Report”) is 
the second ESG Report issued by XtalPi Holdings Limited (the “XtalPi”, 
“we”, “our”, “us” and “Company”), which is designated to give an 
objective and true view of the Company’s strategies, policies, measures 
and achievements in terms of sustainable development, and focuses on 
the disclosure of the Company’s information in environmental, social and 
governance (“ESG”) aspects.
Basis of Preparation
The Report has been prepared pursuant to the Environmental, Social and 
Governance Reporting Code (the “ESG Code”) as set out in Appendix 
C2 to the Rules (the “Listing Rules”) Governing the Listing of Securities 
on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Hong Kong Stock 
Exchange”).
Reporting Cycle
From January 1, 2025 to December 31, 2025 (the “Reporting Period” or 
the “Year”), some related information may extend beyond the Reporting 
Period.
Reporting Scope
The reporting scope of the Report covers XtalPi Holdings Limited and its 
subsidiaries (the “Group”), which is in line with the 2025 annual report of 
the Company.
Source of Information and Guarantee for Reliability
Save as otherwise indicated, data contained herein are derived from the 
internal information, investigation and interview records and relevant 
documents of the Company. The Board of Directors of the Company 
undertakes that the Report does not contain any false information or 
misleading statement, and is responsible for its truthfulness, accuracy and 
completeness.
Confirmation and Approval
The Report has been approved by the Board of Directors on March 25, 
2026 upon confirmation by the management.
Availability
The Report is incorporated in the 2025 annual report of the Company. 
Out of concern for environmental protection, we recommend you to 
read the electronic version that is available on the website of the Stock 
Exchange (www.hkexnews.hk) and the official website of the Company 
(https://ir.xtalpi.com/en/).
關於本報告
《晶泰控股有限公司環境、社會及管治報告》
（以下簡稱「本報告」）是晶泰控股有限公司（以
下簡稱「晶泰科技」、「我們」或「本公司」）發佈
的第二份ESG報告。本報告旨在客觀、真實地
闡述公司在可持續發展方面的策略、政策、措
施及成果，並重點披露公司在環境、社會及管
治（以下簡稱「ESG」）方面的相關信息。
編製依據
本報告編製遵循香港交易所有限公司（以下簡
稱「香港聯交所」）證券上市規則（以下簡稱《上
市規則》）附錄C2所載《環境、社會及管治報告
守則》（以下簡稱《ESG守則》）編製而成。

及報告期外。

Environmental, Social and Governance Report
A LETTER FROM CEO
The year 2025 marks a year of accumulated momentum and 
comprehensive advancement for XtalPi. Against the backdrop of rapid 
and transformative advancements in AI, we have not only witnessed the 
profound impact of AI for Science (AI4S) in redefining global research 
paradigms, but also harnessed the deep integration of “AI + Robotics + 
Multi-Agent” to convert the frontiers of scientific exploration into tangible 
industrial value.
With the unremitting efforts and dedication of all our colleagues, 
XtalPi gained widespread industry recognition and high praise 
for its frontier exploration in this year. Leveraging our data-driven 
R&D innovation capabilities, we won the first prize in the National 
“Data Element ×” Competition. With our accumulated expertise in 
AI and robotics, the Company achieved historic breakthroughs in its 
commercialization landscape in 2025, and attained annual profitability 
for the first time. We entered into a major pipeline collaboration with 
DoveTree, achieving a breakthrough in the scale of AI-driven new drug 
research and development orders. We entered into a multi-target strategic 
collaboration and platform licensing agreement with Eli Lilly, with a total 
deal value of US$345 million, focusing on the development of large￾molecule therapeutics. Moreover, we continued to deepen our partnership 
with international companies including Pfizer, Johnson & Johnson and 
Merck KGaA.
The warmth of our technology is being transformed into life￾saving hope for patients. A number of XtalPi-enabled research 
achievements have achieved milestone breakthroughs, 
underscoring the real-world value of technological innovation.
Among them, SIGX1094, our co-developed targeted drug for diffuse 
gastric cancer with Signet, successfully received the FDA’s Orphan Drug 
Designation (ODD) and Fast Track Designation and was nominated 
for the Prix Galien USA, offering new hope for gastric cancer patients. 
Additionally, RTX-117, the CMT small molecule innovative drug 
empowering ReviR (溪礫科技), has also been granted the Orphan Drug 
Designation (ODD) by the FDA, injecting new momentum into rare disease 
treatment.
首席執行官致辭
2025年，是晶泰科技厚積薄發、全面躍升的
一年。在人工智能跨越式進化的浪潮中，我們
不僅見證了AI for Science (AI4S)重塑全球科研
範式，更以「AI+機器人+Multi-Agent」的深度
融合，將科學探索的邊界轉化為產業價值的實
境。
這一年，在全體同仁的不懈努力與奮力拼搏
下，晶泰科技的前沿探索獲得了業界的廣泛
認可與高度肯定。我們憑藉數據驅動研發的創
新能力，在全國「數據要素×」大賽中斬獲一等
獎。依託在AI與機器人技術領域的積累，2025
年公司商業化版圖實現歷史性突破，更首次實
現年度盈利：與DoveTree達成重要管線合作，
實現AI新藥研發訂單規模的突破；與禮來(Eli 
Lilly)圍繞大分子藥物研發，達成3.45億美元的
多靶點戰略合作及平臺授權協議；持續與輝
瑞、強生、默克等國際企業深化合作。
我們的技術溫度，正轉化為患者的生存希望。
多項由晶泰賦能的科研成果實現里程碑式跨
越，彰顯了技術創新的實際價值。其中，與希
格生科共同推進的SIGX1094彌漫性胃癌靶向
藥，成功獲得美國FDA孤兒藥資格及快速通道
認定並入圍Prix Galien USA（蓋倫獎）提名，有
望為胃癌患者帶來新的治療希望；賦能溪礫科
技的CMT小分子創新藥RTX-117獲FDA孤兒藥
資格認定，為罕見病治療領域的突破注入新動
能。
Co-Founder and Chief Executive Officer 
聯合創始人兼首席執行官
Dr. Ma Jian 
馬健博士

108 XtalPi Holdings Limited Annual Report 2025
環境、社會及管治報告
Environmental, Social and Governance Report
As a practitioner of green technology innovation, XtalPi remained 
committed to its corporate social responsibility, continuously 
focusing on the social and environmental value created by scientific 
and technological progress. Through automated experiments and 
data-driven R&D paradigms, we steadily enhanced resource utilisation 
efficiency, significantly reducing chemical reagent consumption and 
energy waste at the source. We proactively explore the implementation 
paths for sustainable innovation in fields including agriculture, materials, 
energy, and consumer health, contributing to the achievement of “dual 
carbon” goals with the power of technology. Our ESG practices have 
also gained authoritative recognition. Winning the 2025 ESG Pioneer 60 
Annual Enterprise ESG Practice Award, both represented the recognition 
of our past ESG efforts and inspired our future development.
The warmth of technology extends well beyond the laboratory. 
Staying true to our founding mission of “technology for good”, 
we closely integrate corporate development with social welfare 
initiatives. Upon the fire broke out in Hong Kong, the Company 
immediately activated its assistance mechanism and provided emergency 
financial donations to the affected areas, donated computers to the 
Guangdong Provincial Education Foundation for the establishment 
of “Green Caring Computer Classrooms” in schools, leveraged our 
industry-academia-research strengths and hosted the “Future Scientists” 
study camp to popularise AI and drug discovery among teenagers, and 
partnered with welfare foundations to turn poems written by children in 
mountainous regions into AI illustrations, striving to bring the warmth of 
technology to every corner of society.
Looking ahead, XtalPi will continue to explore technological 
innovation, uphold its ESG principles, and work with global 
partners to develop more efficient research models, creating 
greater value for industrial development, social well-being and 
environmental protection.
Co-Founder and Chief Executive Officer
Dr. Ma Jian
作為綠色科技創新的踐行者，晶泰科技牢記企
業社會責任，持續關注科技進步所創造的社會
與環境價值。通過自動化實驗與數據驅動的研
發範式，我們持續提升資源利用效率，從源頭
上大幅減少化學試劑的損耗與能源浪費。我們
在農業、材料、能源、消費健康等領域積極探
索可持續創新的落地路徑，以科技力量助力
「雙碳」目標實現。在ESG領域，公司的實踐也
得到權威認可，榮獲2025 ESG先鋒60年度企
業ESG實踐獎，這是對我們過往ESG工作的肯
定，更是對未來發展的激勵。
科技的溫度，不止於實驗室。我們不忘「科技
向善」的初心，將企業發展與社會公益緊密結
合。在得知香港突發火災後，公司第一時間啟
動援助機制，向受災地區提供緊急資金捐助；
向廣東省教育基金會捐贈電腦，助力學校設立
「綠色愛心電腦教室」；依託產學研優勢舉辦
「未來科學家」研學營，向青少年普及AI與藥物
研發知識；聯動公益基金會，用AI將山區孩子
的詩歌繪製成插畫。我們努力讓科技的溫度抵
達社會每一個角落。
展望未來，晶泰科技將繼續深化技術創新，堅
守ESG理念，與全球合作伙伴共同探索更高效
的科研模式，為產業發展、社會福祉與環境保
護創造更大的價值。

1. ABOUT XTALPI
1.1 About Us
Originating from the MIT campus on the banks of the Charles River, XtalPi 
was founded in 2015 by three physicists. The Company is an innovative 
technology platform company driven by quantum physics, empowered 
by artificial intelligence (AI), and powered by robotics. Since its inception, 
the Company has consistently pursued independent innovation as the 
core driver of its development, and continuously increased investment in 
research and development. By leveraging cutting-edge technologies such 
as quantum physics, artificial intelligence, cloud computing, and robotic 
experiments, the Company has built an innovative R&D platform oriented 
towards future industries. This platform, benefiting from the integration 
of generic technologies, addresses the need to explore the microscopic 
molecular world. It has achieved notable success in the biopharmaceutical 
industry by significantly enhancing R&D efficiency and success rates. 
Its spillover effects have extended to sectors such as new materials, 
chemicals, new energy, agriculture, aiding innovative R&D in new 
materials, promoting the upgrading of the chemical industry, accelerating 
breakthroughs in new energy technologies, and injecting powerful new 
momentum into the development of future industries.
Currently, the XtalPi team is primarily based in Shenzhen, Shanghai, and 
Beijing in China, as well as Boston in the United States and Liverpool in the 
United Kingdom. R&D personnel account for over 70% of the workforce, 
and the total area of its established wet laboratories exceeds 10,000 
square metres. The Company has deployed large-scale, self-developed AI 
robotic experiment workstations and clusters in Shenzhen and Shanghai. 
Grounded in an intelligent platform that integrates AI and robotics, we 
drive a revolution in drug discovery to enhance the length and quality of 
human life. We empower new energy and materials science to expand 
the frontiers of human existence and development. This new R&D 
paradigm, AI × Robotics, is helping us forge a more sustainable, 
greener, and lower-carbon path for human progress.
1. 走進晶泰科技
1.1 關於我們
晶泰科技起源於位於查爾斯河畔的麻省理工學
院(MIT)校園，2015年由三位物理學家創立，
是一家基於量子物理、以人工智能賦能和機器
人驅動的創新平台型科技公司。自創立以來，
公司始終將自主創新作為發展的核心驅動力，
持續加大研發投入，利用量子物理、人工智
能、雲計算、機器人實驗等前沿技術領域打造
了面向未來產業的創新型研發平台。該平台憑
藉共性技術集成優勢，滿足微觀分子世界探索
的需求，在生物醫藥行業成效顯著，大幅提升
了研發效率和成功率，其溢出效應已覆蓋至新
材料、化工、新能源、農業等領域，助力新材
料創新研發，推動化工產業升級，加速新能源
技術突破，為未來產業發展注入澎湃新動力。
目前，晶泰科技團隊主要分佈於中國深圳、上
海、北京以及美國波士頓、英國利物浦，研發
人員佔比超70%，已建成的濕實驗室面積超過
10,000平方米。公司在深圳、上海規模化部署
了自主研發的AI機器人實驗工站集群，以AI與
機器人融合的智能平台為基－我們驅動藥物研
發革命，提高人類生命的長度和質量；我們賦
能新能源與材料科學，拓展人類生存和發展的
邊疆。AI×機器人研發新範式，正在幫助我們
走出一條更可持續、更綠色、更低碳的人類發
展之路。

Environmental, Social and Governance Report
2. LAYING SOLID FOUNDATIONS AND ACTING 
WITH DILIGENCE: THE CORNERSTONE OF 
GOVERNANCE
XtalPi adheres to integrity and compliance, actively constructing a 
transparent and efficient corporate governance system, and deeply 
integrating ESG principles into its operations. Grounded in technological 
innovation and social responsibility, we transform our technological 
strengths into social value. Through advocating for transparent governance 
and stable operation, we aim to achieve long-term coexistence and shared 
prosperity for the Company and society.
2.1 ESG Governance
ESG Management Philosophy
We adhere to the philosophy of “remaining true to our original aspirations 
and aiming high”, deeply committing to our ‘AI for Science’ strategic 
direction. By focusing on core human needs such as healthcare and 
energy, we translate societal concerns into the initiatives for technological 
innovation and product development, thereby building distinctive core 
competitiveness and technological barriers amidst the wave of artificial 
intelligence. In March 2026, the Company received an MSCI ESG rating 
of “A” for the first time, marking international recognition of its phased 
achievements.
ESG Governance Structure
To systematically advance the sustainable development strategy, the 
Company has established an ESG governance structure led by the Board 
of Directors, with clearly defined roles and responsibilities across levels. By 
adopting the Environmental, Social and Governance (ESG) Working Group 
Charter, a cross-functional collaboration mechanism has been established 
to ensure the effective implementation of ESG initiatives. The ESG Working 
Group reports regularly to the Board of Directors on progress, providing a 
basis for management decision-making.
2. 築基篤行 • 治理基石
晶泰科技堅持誠信合規，積極構建透明高效的
公司治理體系，將ESG理念深度融入運營。我
們以科技創新與社會責任為根本，將技術優勢
轉化為社會價值，並通過倡導陽光治理與穩健
運營，實現企業與社會的長遠共生共榮。
2.1 ESG管治
ESG管理理念
我們堅持「不忘初心，志存高遠」的理念，深耕
AI for Science戰略方向，聚焦醫藥、能源等人
類核心需求，將社會關切轉化為技術創新與產
品研發方向，在人工智能浪潮中構築獨具特色
的核心競爭力與技術壁壘。2026年3月，公司
首次MSCI ESG評級為「A」，階段性成果獲國際
認可。
ESG管治架構
為系統推進可持續發展戰略，公司構建了董
事會主導的ESG治理架構，明確劃分各層級權
責。通過《環境、社會與管治(ESG)工作組章
程》，建立跨層級協作機制，確保ESG工作高
效落地。ESG工作組定期向董事會匯報進展，
為管理層決策提供依據。

Environmental, Social and Governance Report
Board of Directors
董事會
As the highest responsible body for the management and public disclosure of the 
Company’s ESG matters, the Board of Directors bears ultimate responsibility and 
primarily exercises the following functions:
• Reviewing the materiality of risks and issues related to the Company’s ESG matters;
• Reviewing and approving the Company’s sustainability strategy and goals;
• Overseeing and reviewing the Company’s policies, management, performance, and progress 
towards related goals in ESG matters; and
• Reviewing and approving the public disclosure of the Company’s performance on ESG-related 
matters.
董事會作為公司ESG事宜管理及公開披露的最高責任機構，承擔最終責任，主要行使以下職能：
• 審議本公司ESG事宜相關風險及議題重要性；
• 審議、批准公司可持續發展戰略與目標；
• 監督、檢討本公司ESG事宜相關政策、管理、表現及相關目標進度；
• 審議、批准本公司就ESG相關事宜表現的公開披露。
ESG Working Group
ESG工作組
Comprised of the Company’s senior management and heads of various business and 
functional departments, the ESG Working Group serves as the primary supervisory and 
coordinating body, primarily exercising the following functions and providing support 
to the Board:
• Allocating resources for the implementation of the Company’s ESG strategy and the 
achievement of ESG goals;
• Formulating ESG policies and action plans that align with the Company’s sustainability 
strategy and objectives;
• Managing ESG-related risks in the Company’s day-to-day operations;
• Communicating with relevant departments, branches and subsidiaries of the Company to 
coordinate and advance the implementation of ESG initiatives; and
• Reviewing the public disclosure of the Company’s performance on ESG-related matters.
ESG工作組由公司高管以及各業務、職能部門負責人組成，作為主要監督及協調機構，主要行
使以下職能並為董事會提供支持：
• 為公司ESG策略的實施及ESG目標的實現分配資源；
• 制定符合本公司可持續發展戰略與目標的ESG事宜相關政策及行動計劃；
• 管理本公司日常運營過程的ESG相關風險；
• 負責與本公司相關部門、分子公司的溝通，協調推進ESG相關事宜落地執行；
• 審閱本公司就ESG相關事宜表現的公開披露。
Relevant Business and 
Functional Departments
相關業務及職能部門
As the primary implementation level, relevant personnel in each department primarily 
exercise the following functions and provide support to the ESG Working Group:
• Implementing the Company’s ESG strategy and executing the ESG action plan;
• Addressing ESG-related risks in the department’s day-to-day operations; and
• Handling other ESG-related matters.
作為主要執行層級，各部門相關人員主要行使以下職能並為ESG工作組提供支持：
• 貫徹公司ESG戰略，執行ESG行動計劃；
• 應對部門日常運營過程的ESG相關風險；
• 其他ESG相關事宜。

Environmental, Social and Governance Report
Statement of the Board of Directors
As the Company’s core governance body, the Board of Directors assumes 
comprehensive responsibility for supervising, guiding, and reviewing 
ESG-related work. The Board of Directors is responsible for overseeing 
the implementation of the Company’s overall ESG strategy and the 
effectiveness of ESG risk management, ensuring the Company’s steady 
progress on its sustainable development path. To efficiently advance the 
ESG efforts, the Board authorises the ESG Working Group to formulate 
ESG strategies, targets, and policies, and to monitor the implementation 
progress of ESG-related matters. In the Company’s daily operations, the 
Board of Directors regularly reviews progress towards the Company’s 
various ESG targets.
To implement various ESG agendas, the Company has established an 
ESG Working Group, comprised of the Company’s senior management 
and heads of various business and functional departments. This group is 
responsible for coordinating and managing various ESG communication 
efforts, promoting the concrete execution of the Company’s ESG 
strategies and policies, and is dedicated to integrating ESG management 
concepts into all aspects of the Company’s daily operations, ensuring 
that sustainable development targets are closely aligned with business 
development. Concurrently, through regular and diverse promotional 
activities, we aim to comprehensively enhance the ESG knowledge base 
and sustainability awareness of all employees.
To effectively prevent and control internal and external risks, the Board 
of Directors is responsible for supervising material ESG issues and 
identifying potential risks and opportunities. The Board of Directors is 
responsible for reviewing and approving risks to be disclosed, formulating 
countermeasures in advance to better manage ESG risks and mitigate their 
negative impact on the Company’s operations.
董事會聲明
董事會作為公司的核心治理機構，全面承擔公
司ESG相關工作的監督、指導及檢討責任。董
事會負責監管公司整體ESG戰略的落實情況及
ESG風險管理的有效性，確保公司在可持續發
展道路上穩步前行。為高效推進ESG工作，董
事會授權ESG工作組制定ESG戰略、目標、政
策並監察ESG相關事務的實施進度。在公司日
常運營中，董事會定期檢討公司各項ESG目標
達成進展。
為貫徹落實各項ESG議程，本公司設立ESG工
作組，由公司高管以及各業務、職能部門負責
人組成。該小組負責協調統籌各項ESG溝通工
作，推動公司ESG策略及政策的具體執行，致
力於將ESG管理理念融入公司日常運營的方方
面面，確保可持續發展目標與業務發展緊密結
合。同時，通過常態化、多元化的宣貫活動，
全面提升全員ESG知識儲備與可持續發展意
識。
為有效防控內外部風險，董事會負責對重要
ESG議題進行監督，識別潛在風險與機遇。董
事會負責審查和批准將要披露的風險，提前制
定應對措施以更好地管理ESG風險，緩解其對
公司運營產生的負面影響。

Materiality Analysis
We conduct a materiality assessment annually to identify issues of 
high importance to the Company, guiding sustainable development 
management and information disclosure efforts, and better meeting the 
expectations and demands of stakeholders. During the Reporting Period, 
we reviewed the prioritisation of 21 material issues, and the Board of 
Directors approved the annual review results. Compared to the previous 
reporting year, there were no changes to the list of material issues this 
year.

環境、社會及管治報告
Environmental, Social and Governance Report
2.2 Integrity and Honesty
The Company strictly adheres to the laws and regulations of the 
jurisdictions in which it operates, including the Anti-Unfair Competition 
Law of the People’s Republic of China (《中華人民共和國反不正當競
爭法》) and the Anti-Monopoly Law of the People’s Republic of China
(《中華人民共和國反壟斷法》), and maintains a zero-tolerance policy 
towards commercial bribery, unfair competition, and similar acts. We have 
established internal policies such as the Anti-Bribery and Anti-Corruption 
Management System and the Anti-Fraud Management System, and 
require all employees to sign the Letter of Commitment of Anti-corruption 
and Self-discipline, effectively managing risks related to business ethics 
and anti-corruption.
2.2 誠實守信
公司恪守《中華人民共和國反不正當競爭法》
《中華人民共和國反壟斷法》等運營地法律法
規，對商業賄賂、不正當競爭等行為秉持零容
忍態度。我們已制定《反賄賂反腐敗管理制度》
《反舞弊管理制度》等內部制度，並要求所有入
職員工簽署《廉潔自律承諾書》，有效防控商業
道德與反貪污風險。
• Responsible for overseeing management in establishing a company-wide culture 
of anti-bribery and anti-corruption, and for establishing and improving the internal 
control system;
• Authorised the Audit Committee to provide guidance and supervision regarding the 
company’s anti-bribery and anti-corruption work.
• 負責督促管理層建立公司範圍內的反賄賂反腐敗文化環境，建立健全內部控制體系；
• 授權審核委員會對公司反賄賂反腐敗工作進行指導和監督。
• Responsible for establishing, refining and effectively implementing internal control 
mechanisms to minimise the likelihood of bribery and corruption, and for taking 
appropriate and effective remedial measures in response to such acts, whilst accepting 
supervision from the Board of Directors and the Audit Committee.
• 負責建立、健全並有效實施內部控制機制，以降低賄賂腐敗行為發生的機會，並對
賄賂腐敗行為採取適當有效的補救措施，接受董事會、審核委員會的監督。
• Assist with the investigation and evidence-gathering in cases of bribery and 
corruption;
• Be responsible for the integrity and self-discipline management of staff within the 
department, as well as the signing and implementation of the Letter of Commitment 
of Anti-corruption and Self-discipline by staff.
• 配合賄賂腐敗事件的調查和取證工作；
• 負責本部門員工廉潔自律管理以及員工《廉潔自律承諾書》簽署執行落實。
• Handling reports of relevant misconduct and organising case investigations;
• Formulating recommendations for case handling and accountability, and reporting 
these to the Management and the Audit Committee;
• Organising staff to sign the Letter of Commitment of Anti-corruption and Self￾discipline;
• Organising awareness-raising activities and training on anti-corruption and anti￾bribery legislation;
• Other tasks related to anti-bribery and anti-corruption.
• 受理相關行為的舉報工作，組織開展案件調查；
• 對案件提出處理意見和責任追究意見並向管理層、審核委員會報告；
• 組織員工簽署《廉潔自律承諾書》；
• 組織宣傳反腐敗反賄賂的法律規定及學習；
• 其他反賄賂反腐敗相關工作。
The Board of Directors
董事會
Management
管理層
Risk Control and Audit 
Department
風控審計部
Each Business and 
Functional Department
各業務與職能部門
Business Ethics and Anti-corruption Management Structure of XtalPi
晶泰科技商業道德及反貪腐管理架構

[PDF PAGE 129]
128 XtalPi Holdings Limited Annual Report 2025
環境、社會及管治報告
Environmental, Social and Governance Report
Through regular business ethics audits and assessments, we continuously 
monitor compliance in key areas. In 2025, the Company completed several 
special audits, including the Foreign Corrupt Practices Act (FCPA) (《反海外
腐敗法》) compliance assessment, as well as reviews of the sales process, 
inventory management, and procurement of third-party experimental 
services. No instances of corruption, bribery, or unfair competition were 
identified.
Furthermore, we continuously cultivate a corporate culture of 
integrity and uprightness. New employees are required to study the 
anti-bribery and anti-corruption requirements and pass an examination, 
ensuring compliance awareness is firmly established from the start of 
their employment. Concurrently, we published numerous thematic 
communications through the “Sunshine XtalPi” internal subscription 
channel, covering topics such as conflict of interest declarations, 
interpretation of the Letter of Commitment of Anti-corruption and Self￾discipline, and guidelines for gift declaration. This helps employees clarify 
behavioural boundaries, enhance their awareness of ethical conduct, 
and foster an internal atmosphere of openness, transparency, and 
self-discipline.
In August 2025, the Company invited external experts to conduct a 
specialised training session on the US Foreign Corrupt Practices Act (《反
海外腐敗法》) for all directors, delving into core provisions, typical cases, 
and key risk prevention points. Highlights from this training were shared 
with all employees on International Anti-Corruption Day (9 December), 
achieving a simultaneous deepening of compliance awareness from the 
Board of Directors down to all staff.
Whistle-blowing and Complaints
To enhance internal oversight mechanisms, we have established a 
standardised, confidential, and independent Whistle-blowing and 
Investigation Policy, and have set up diverse and accessible channels 
for reporting violations. Through internal communications and public 
announcements on the Company’s official website, we ensure that 
employees and partners are aware of and know how to use these 
whistleblowing channels effectively. All employees and business partners 
can report violations via online links, dedicated email addresses, dedicated 
telephone hotlines, and physical mail.
我們通過定期開展商業道德審計與評估，持
續監督關鍵領域的合規狀況。在2025年，公
司已完成包括FCPA (Foreign Corrupt Practices 
Act)合規評估以及銷售流程、存貨管理、第三
方實驗服務採購在內的多項專項檢查，未發現
涉及貪污腐敗或不正當競爭的情況。
此外，我們持續建設廉潔正直的企業文化。新
入職員工通過學習反腐敗反賄賂相關要求並完
成考試，確保合規意識從入職伊始便牢固建
立。同時，以「陽光晶泰」內部訂閱號渠道發佈

員工明確行為邊界，增強廉潔從業意識，形成
公開、透明、自律的內部氛圍。
2025年8月，公司特邀外部專家為全體董事開

2.3 Stable Operations
Risk Management
Through policies such as the Measures for Risk Management, we have 
established a systematic response mechanism, continuously enhancing the 
Company’s adaptability in uncertain environments. We have established 
a three-tier risk management organisational structure led by the Board 
of Directors, ensuring the scientific nature of risk decisions and the 
effectiveness of implementation through clear division of responsibilities 
and reporting lines.
■ Board of Directors: As the highest level of risk management, it 
is responsible for formulating the overall risk management and 
control strategy and targets.
■ Audit Committee: As a key intermediate level, it comprehensively 
supervises the implementation of risk management work, ensuring 
the effective execution of various measures.
■ Departments: As the implementation level, they specifically execute 
risk control measures, ensuring that risk management is integrated 
into daily operations.
We have established a systematic risk management process covering risk 
identification, assessment, response, and reporting, effectively ensuring 
the orderly conduct of risk control work and supporting the Company’s 
stable operations.
我們堅決杜絕同類違規問題再次發生，通過系
統化的改進機制，在事件發生後同步推進及時
補救、案例復盤與流程檢討，並將經驗轉化為
制度與工作機制的優化，從而構建從應對到預
防的管理閉環，降低違規事件重複發生風險。
報告期內，本集團未發生涉及貪污腐敗或不正
當競爭的重大訴訟案件。
2.3 穩健運營
風險管理
我們通過《風險管理辦法》等制度建立系統化應
對機制，持續提升企業在不確定環境中的適應
力。我們已建立由董事會領導的三級風險管理
組織架構，通過清晰的權責劃分與報告路徑，
保障風險決策的科學性與執行的有效性。
■ 董事會：作為風險管理的最高層級，負
責制定整體的風險管控策略和目標。
■ 審核委員會：作為中間關鍵層級，全面
監管風險管理工作的開展，確保各項措
施得到有效落實。
■ 各部門：作為執行層級，具體執行風險
管控措施，確保風險管理工作貫穿於日
常運營中。
我們建立了從風險識別、評估、應對到報告的
系統化風險管理流程，有效保障風險管控工作
有序開展，支撐企業穩健經營。

Information Security Protection
The Company strictly complies with laws and regulations such as the 
Cybersecurity Law of the People’s Republic of China (《中華人民共和國網
絡安全法》) and the Data Security Law of the People’s Republic of China 
(《中華人民共和國數據安全法》). Benchmarking against the requirements 
of internationally advanced Information Security Management Systems 
(ISMS) and taking into account actual business needs, we have 
systematically established an information security policy framework. We 
continuously strengthen the confidentiality, integrity and availability of 
information assets, guard against cybersecurity risks, and provide a secure 
foundation for stable business operations.
We have established an Information Security Management Committee, 
with the CEO serving as the highest responsible person overseeing 
information security-related work across the Group. The Information 
Security Management Committee Charter clearly defines the roles and 
responsibilities of the committee, management teams, execution teams 
and supervision teams at various levels, ensuring the implementation of 
management requirements.
During the Reporting Period, the Company engaged a third-party 
organisation to conduct an information security risk assessment, providing 
professional opinions for the continuous improvement of the Company’s 
information security. Concurrently, the Company successfully passed the 
ISO 27001 Information Security Management System audit and obtained 
certification, marking authoritative recognition of its information security 
management capabilities.

3. SUPPORTING OUR CLIENTS·VALUE CO￾CREATION
Adhering to the AI for Science philosophy, the Company continuously 
enhances its technological capabilities and management mechanisms 
focusing on R&D innovation, quality assurance and customer service. 
Guided by market demands and centred on customer value, it provides 
stable, reliable and high-quality solutions for various industries.
3.1 Pioneering the Future
Innovation is a key driving force for the Company’s business development. 
Building on its professional talent pool and scientific research foundation, 
the Company has established an innovative R&D platform supported by 
quantum physics and integrated with artificial intelligence and robotics 
technologies. Leveraging first-principles calculation, AI models, high￾performance cloud computing, and scalable, standardised robotic 
automation systems, it provides comprehensive R&D services covering 
drug discovery, synthesis, solid-state R&D and intelligent experimentation 
for the biomedical, materials science and other fields, facilitating the 
upgrading of research paradigms towards intelligence and systematisation. 
In 2025, the Company’s R&D investment amounted to RMB569.2 million.

質量保障與客戶服務，持續完善技術能力與管
理機制，堅持以市場需求為導向、客戶價值為
核心，為各行業提供穩定可靠的高品質解決方
案。
3.1 領創未來
創新是公司業務發展的重要驅動力。依託專業
人才與科研基礎，公司構建以量子物理為理論
支撐、融合人工智能與機器人技術的創新研發
平台，運用第一性原理計算、AI模型、高性能
雲計算及可擴展、標準化機器人自動化系統，
為生物醫藥、材料科學等領域提供覆蓋藥物發
現、合成、固體形態研發及智能實驗的綜合
研發服務，助力科研模式向智能化、系統化升
級。2025年，公司研發投入為人民幣569.2百
萬元。
創新生態建設
公司持續推進創新生態建設，通過強化研發能
力與拓展多方協作，營造有利於技術創新與成
果轉化的良好環境。

平的不斷提升。報告期內，公司圍繞研發能力
建設開展以下工作：

3.2 Intellectual property protection
Intellectual Property Management System
We strictly comply with the Patent Law of the People’s Republic of 
China (《中華人民共和國專利法》), the Copyright Law of the People’s 
Republic of China (《中華人民共和國著作權法》), the Trademark Law of 
the People’s Republic of China (《中華人民共和國商標法》) and other 
applicable laws and regulations in the regions where we operate. Focusing 
on key aspects including the creation, application, commercialization 
and protection of intellectual property, we have continuously optimised 
institutional documents including the Intellectual Property Management 
System and the Intellectual Property Incentive Measures, providing 
guidance for the standardised operation of intellectual property work. 
Thanks to the Company’s sound intellectual property management system, 
we have successfully passed the certification of the GB/T 29490 Enterprise 
Intellectual Property Management System, providing a solid guarantee for 
the continued advancement of technological innovation and achievement 
transformation.

礎。我們以自主創新為引領，深化前沿技術研
究，推動技術能力與實際應用場景的緊密結

動化實驗及生物醫藥等方向持續取得階段性研

年度報告 2025 晶泰控股有限公司 139
環境、社會及管治報告
Environmental, Social and Governance Report
Management Measures
Focusing on potential intellectual property risks involved in innovative 
R&D activities, we prioritise risk identification and prevention and control 
as key links in intellectual property management, establishing a regular 
monitoring mechanism covering the front-end of R&D and business 
operations. Relying on professional tools such as patent databases and 
trademark databases, the Company continuously tracks intellectual 
property information related to its core technologies and brands. On one 
hand, it monitors potential infringement acts by external entities; on the 
other hand, it identifies and assesses potential third-party intellectual 
property risks in the Company’s own R&D and business operations.
For identified potential risks, the intellectual property team takes the lead, 
working with R&D, branding and other teams to promptly formulate 
response plans based on specific risk scenarios. By adjusting R&D paths, 
improving usage standards or adopting necessary protective measures, the 
possibility of intellectual property disputes is reduced, thereby continuously 
enhancing the forward-looking and effectiveness of intellectual property 
risk prevention and control, and providing support for the steady 
advancement of business and long-term development.
Certificate of Enterprise Intellectual Property Management System Certification
企業知識產權管理體系認證證書
管理舉措
圍繞創新研發活動中可能涉及的知識產權風
險，我們將風險識別與防控作為知識產權管理
工作的重點環節，建立覆蓋研發前端與業務運
營的常態化監測機制。公司依託專利數據庫、
商標數據庫等專業工具，對涉及公司核心技術
和品牌的知識產權信息進行持續跟蹤，一方面
關注外部主體可能存在的侵權行為，另一方面
對公司自身研發和經營活動中潛在的第三方知
識產權風險進行識別與評估。
對於發現的潛在風險，由知識產權團隊牽頭，
與研發、品牌等團隊共同根據具體風險情形及
時制定應對方案，通過調整研發路徑、完善使
用規範或採取必要的保護措施，降低知識產權
糾紛發生的可能性，以此持續提升知識產權風
險防控的前瞻性與有效性，為業務穩健推進和
長期發展提供支持。

3.3 Quality First
Quality management system
We regard the quality of products and services as a crucial foundation 
for supporting the steady development of business and customer trust, 
and continuously improve the quality management system covering the 
entire process of R&D, delivery and operations. The Company strictly 
complies with the national safety standard for electrical equipment GB 
47931 and the requirements of the ISO 9001 quality management system, 
establishing a management structure consisting of the top management 
(CEO) – Quality Management Representative – Quality Internal Auditors 
of each department. With the Quality Manual as the guiding principle, 
supplemented by comprehensive secondary documents and work 
instructions, the Company refines quality requirements to specific 
operational levels, promoting the standardised operation and continuous 
improvement of quality management work. During the Reporting Period, 
the Company completed the revision and optimization of 25 quality 
system documents.

3.4 客戶服務
在市場推廣與品牌傳播過程中，我們嚴格遵循
法律法規，規範開展營銷活動，持續鞏固客戶
信任並維護企業聲譽。我們高度重視客戶體
驗，以提升服務感知與響應效率目標，以《商
務管理制度》為基礎構建了職責清晰、流程規
範的客戶服務保障機制，確保持續優化客戶整
體服務體驗。
客戶滿意度調查
公司建立客戶滿意度管理機制，持續收集、分
析並反饋客戶意見。相關意見經整理後及時傳
遞至業務與商務拓展團隊，用於制定改進措施
並形成定期匯總分析，推動客戶需求在業務執
行中的閉環響應。
我們持續優化客戶滿意度收集方式與問卷設
計，圍繞業務實際場景完善調研內容，並引入
紛享銷客系統問卷功能，通過二維碼形式提升
客戶反饋便利性，問卷回覆效率大幅提升。同
時，針對部分業務線由原按項目發送問卷調整
為按客戶維度季度統一發送，在減少重複打擾
的同時擴大覆蓋範圍，有效提升客戶體驗。
Relying on the audit mechanism, the Company systematically identifies 
problems in operation and promptly initiates rectification processes 
for identified issues to achieve closed-loop management. During the 
rectification process, we simultaneously conduct internal reviews and 
process optimization, trace the root causes of problems, improve relevant 
management measures, and continuously enhance system execution and 
quality management maturity.
We integrate quality culture building into the daily management system, 
and strengthen employees’ quality awareness and operational standards 
through continuous training. During the Reporting Period, various business 
segments of the Company organised a total of 23 hours of quality-related 
training, covering 265 employees. Systematic learning was carried out 
focusing on quality process management, standardised use of equipment 
and identification of quality issues, promoting the implementation of 
quality management concepts at the frontline of business.
3.4 Client Service
In the process of marketing and brand communication, the Company 
strictly complies with laws and regulations, conducts marketing activities 
in a standardised manner, and continuously consolidates customer trust 
and maintains corporate reputation. We attach great importance to 
customer experience, aiming to enhance service perception and response 
efficiency. Based on the Business Management System, we have established 
a customer service guarantee mechanism with clear responsibilities and 
standardised processes to ensure the continuous optimization of the 
overall customer service experience.
Client satisfaction survey
The Company has established a customer satisfaction management 
mechanism to continuously collect, analyse and feedback customer 
opinions. After collation, relevant opinions are promptly transmitted to the 
business and business development teams for formulating improvement 
measures and conducting regular summary and analysis, promoting the 
closed-loop response to customer needs in business execution.
We continuously optimise the customer satisfaction collection methods 
and questionnaire design, improve the research content around actual 
business scenarios, and introduce the questionnaire function of the 
Funshare CX platform. The QR code format enhances the convenience 
of customer feedback, with the questionnaire response efficiency 
significantly increased. Meanwhile, for some business lines, the original 
project-based questionnaire distribution has been adjusted to quarterly 
unified distribution by customer dimension, which expands the coverage 
while reducing repeated interruptions, effectively improving the customer 
experience.

負責任營銷

地適用法律法規，建立完善的營銷管理機制，
並結合公司發展戰略和經營目標組織開展市場
推廣活動。
為保障對外傳播內容真實、準確且符合法律法
規要求，對外宣傳及營銷材料內容須經公共事
務、法務、知識產權等相關專業團隊審查後方
可發佈。我們開展面向員工的負責任溝通與品
牌合規相關學習活動，強化營銷及相關崗位人
員對傳播規範和合規要求的理解。相關內容通
過專題分享、資料開放及入職學習等方式融入
日常能力建設過程，推動員工在對外交流中保
持信息表達的準確性與一致性，持續維護公司
品牌形象與市場信譽。報告期內，公司未發生
因營銷活動引發的訴訟事項。
In 2025, customer satisfaction questionnaires were distributed, and all 
collected responses indicated customer ratings of “satisfactory” or higher 
ratings. The overall feedback remained stable and positive, with no 
abnormal negative comments. During the Reporting Period, the Company 
was awarded the “Technology Innovation Award” as an Outstanding 
Supplier of 2025 by Yangtze River Pharmaceutical Group.
In response to the continuous expansion of the customer base for 
the Company’s new business lines, we have established a full-process 
customer complaint management mechanism covering multi-channel 
collection, hierarchical disposal, and traceability filing, promoting the 
efficient resolution of issues and experience accumulation. The logistics￾related issues of this business reached a closed-loop rate of 100%, with 
a customer satisfaction score of 96, and no complaints related to product 
quality or services were recorded.
Responsible marketing
In accordance with applicable laws and regulations of the places of 
operation such as the Advertising Law of the People’s Republic of China
(《中華人民共和國廣告法》), the Company has established a sound 
marketing management mechanism and organises marketing activities in 
line with the Company’s development strategy and business objectives.
To ensure that external communication content is true, accurate and 
compliant with legal and regulatory requirements, all external publicity 
and marketing materials must be reviewed by relevant professional 
teams including Public Affairs, Legal, and Intellectual Property before 
release. We carry out learning activities on responsible communication 
and brand compliance for employees to enhance the understanding of 
communication norms and compliance requirements among marketing 
and related positions. Relevant content is integrated into daily capacity￾building through thematic sharing, open materials, and onboarding 
learning, promoting employees to maintain the accuracy and consistency 
of information expression in external communications and continuously 
safeguarding the Company’s brand image and market reputation. During 
the Reporting Period, the Company did not incur any litigation arising 
from marketing activities.

systematic framework of supplier management systems to implement 
full-process management of procurement activities. By strengthening 
communication, collaboration and process supervision, we promote 
partners to jointly follow the principles of integrity and compliant business 
practices.

供應商准入

與可持續性。
供應商風險管理
公司依據《供應商管理辦法》對供應商運營風險
開展風險識別、評估與分級管控，及時發現潛
在隱患並制定應對方案，保障供應鏈穩定連
續。同時，結合業務實際運營視角優化風險分
類，重點關注生產經營、員工管理以及環境安
全等維度，並通過《供應商風險評估表》實施常
態化跟蹤管理，實現風險識別日常化，提升前
置發現與過程管控能力。

Supply Chain Risk Control Measures
供應鏈風險管控措施
Supplier onboarding
In the supplier onboarding process, the Company conducts a 
comprehensive assessment from multiple dimensions including product 
and service level, delivery capability, service support and corporate ESG 
performance, and incorporates indicators such as quality, environment, 
health and safety and laboratory 5S into the quantitative supplier 
evaluation. Meanwhile, we conduct prudent screening through 
qualification review and on-site inspections to ensure that potential 
partners meet the Company’s requirements for quality, efficiency and 
compliant operations, safeguarding the stability and sustainability of the 
supply chain from the source.
Supplier risk management
In accordance with the Supplier Management Measures, the Company 
identifies, assesses and implements hierarchical control over suppliers’ 
operational risks, promptly detects potential hazards and formulates 
response plans to ensure the stability and continuity of the supply chain. 
Meanwhile, we optimise risk classification from the perspective of actual 
business operations, focusing on dimensions such as production and 
operation, employee management and environmental safety. Through the 
regular tracking and management via the Supplier Risk Assessment Form, 
we realise daily risk identification and enhance the capability of proactive 
detection and process control.

供應商績效評估
公司建立供應商績效管理機制，圍繞產品質
量、成本控制、交付能力、服務水平及技術
支持等方面開展綜合評價。本年度，公司完
成績效評估流程與工具系統升級，引入X-SRM 
(Xtalpi Supplier Relationship Management)供應
商績效管理平台，對參評人員邀請、線上獨立
評分及結果審批等關鍵環節實行統一管理，進
一步規範評價過程、保障數據記錄可靠、強化
管理決策支撐。
此外，公司同步優化績效評價指標體系，新增
信息安全管理、環境管理以及職業健康與安全
管理等內容，使供應商評價更貼合公司合規經
營與可持續發展要求，進一步提升供應鏈整體
治理水平。
供應商溝通
為保障供應鏈運行的穩定性與協同效率，公司
建立了涵蓋交流、培訓與幫扶的供應商協同管
理體系，通過線上線下多種形式建立常態化的
溝通與反饋渠道，針對質量表現、交付情況及
合規要求開展評估與改進，以促進合作關係不
斷優化。同時，公司組織技術交流與系統培
訓，支持供應商提升專業能力和管理水平，增
強供應鏈整體協同效能。
Supplier performance evaluation
The Company has established a supplier performance management 
mechanism to conduct comprehensive evaluations covering product 
quality, cost control, delivery capability, service level and technical 
support. During the current year, the Company completed the upgrade 
of the performance evaluation process and tool system, introducing 
the X-SRM (XtalPi Supplier Relationship Management) platform. This 
platform implements unified management of key links such as invitation 
of evaluators, online independent scoring and result approval, further 
standardising the evaluation process, ensuring reliable data recording and 
strengthening support for management decisions.
In addition, the Company further optimised the performance evaluation 
indicator system, adding content such as information security 
management, environmental management, and occupational health and 
safety management. This makes the supplier evaluation more in line with 
the Company’s requirements for compliant operations and sustainable 
development, and further improves the overall governance level of the 
supply chain.
Supplier Communication
To ensure the stability and collaborative efficiency of the supply chain 
operation, the Company has established a supplier collaborative 
management system covering communication, training and capacity 
building. Through various online and offline forms, it has established 
regular communication and feedback channels, conducting evaluations 
and improvements on quality performance, delivery status and compliance 
requirements to continuously optimise cooperative relationships. 
Meanwhile, the Company organises technical exchanges and system 
training to support suppliers in improving their professional capabilities and 
management levels, and enhance the overall collaborative effectiveness of 
the supply chain.

隊圍繞採購流程規範組織專項評估，完成兩輪
考核，進一步提升採購管理規範性與風險防控
能力。
Sustainable supply chain
While promoting the collaborative development of the supply chain, the 
Company incorporates sustainable development requirements into key 
considerations of supplier management. Through institutional constraints 
and code of conduct, we define basic standards for suppliers in terms of 
environmental protection, social responsibility performance and corporate 
governance, and encourage key suppliers to sign the Supplier Code of 
Conduct for Anti-corruption Cooperation, so as to promote a standardised, 
transparent and sustainable supply chain.
In the supplier onboarding process, the Company focuses on reviewing 
suppliers’ management capabilities and system certifications in quality, 
environment, occupational health and safety. Meanwhile, the Company 
continuously strengthens the integrity and compliance management of 
procurement activities, requiring procurement personnel to sign the Letter 
of Commitment of Anti-corruption and Self-discipline and conducting 
anti-corruption training to enhance risk prevention awareness and 
institutional implementation capacity. During the Reporting Period, the 
procurement team organised special assessments on procurement process 
standardisation and completed two rounds of reviews, further improving 
the standardisation of procurement management and risk prevention 
capabilities.

4.2 行業共榮
公司堅持開放協同，積極推進產業交流與創新
合作，通過組織和參與醫藥領域專業會議、學
術論壇及行業研討，推動前沿技術經驗共享與
跨界合作，並依託國際展會等平台拓展全球交
流渠道，深化與海外市場和合作夥伴的連接。
以下選取具有代表性的相關案例予以展示。
深化產學研協同創新
Hosting “New Paradigm of Synthetic Chemistry Research – Robotics Integrated with AI Symposium” to Promote 
Academic and Industrial Exchanges
主辦「合成化學研究新範式－機器人融合AI研討會」，推動學術與產業交流
In June 2025, XtalPi hosted the “New Paradigm of Synthetic Chemistry Research – Robotics Integrated with AI 
Symposium” in Shanghai. Academician Ma Shengming from Fudan University served as the Chairman of the Symposium. 
Experts and scholars from universities, research organisations and industrial institutions including Tsinghua University and 
Peking University participated in the exchanges, with more than 300 offline attendees. The Symposium focused on the 
integrated application of robotics and artificial intelligence in the field of synthetic chemistry, and conducted in-depth 
discussions on data-driven R&D and intelligent paradigms. During the Symposium, the Company demonstrated how its 
self-developed intelligent autonomous experimentation platform improves the efficiency of chemical space exploration.
2025年6月，晶泰科技在上海主辦「合成化學研究新範式－機器人融合AI研討會」。會議由復旦大學麻生明院士擔任大會
主席，清華大學、北京大學等高校、科研機構和產業機構的專家學者參與交流，線下參會者逾300人。會議聚焦機器人
技術與人工智能在合成化學領域的融合應用，深入探討了數據驅動研發及智能化範式。期間，公司展示了自主研發的智
能自主實驗平台如何提升化學空間探索效率。
This Symposium provided an exchange platform for all sectors of industry, university and research, promoted cross￾border cooperation among artificial intelligence, robotics and synthetic chemistry research, and offered practical exchange 
opportunities for advancing the new data-driven scientific research paradigm.
本次研討會為產學研各界提供了交流平台，促進了人工智能、機器人技術與合成化學研究的跨界合作，為推動數據驅動
的科研新範式提供了實踐交流機會。
New Paradigm of Synthetic Chemistry Research – Robotics Integrated with AI Symposium
合成化學研究新範式－機器人融合AI研討會

Hosting the 2026 International Symposium on Molecular Glue Drug Discovery to Build a Joint Innovation 
Ecosystem for Molecular Glues
舉辦2026分子膠藥物研發國際研討會，共建分子膠創新生態
In January 2026, the 2026 International Symposium on Molecular Glue Drug Discovery, hosted by XtalPi, was held in 
Shanghai. Experts, scholars and industry representatives from leading domestic and overseas research institutions and 
biomedical enterprises attended the event, including Stanford University, Dana-Farber Cancer Institute, École Polytechnique 
Fédérale de Lausanne (EPFL), Shanghai Institute of Organic Chemistry, Chinese Academy of Sciences, and ShanghaiTech 
University, engaging in in-depth discussions on cutting-edge R&D developments.
2026年1月，由晶泰科技主辦的「2026分子膠藥物研發國際研討會」在上海召開，來自斯坦福大學、Dana-Farber癌症研究
所、瑞士洛桑聯邦理工學院(EPFL)、中國科學院上海有機化學研究所、上海科技大學等海內外科研機構及多家生物醫藥
企業的專家學者與產業代表參會，深度探討研發前沿。
At the symposium, XtalPi shared its XGlue™ molecular glue R&D platform built on AI and automation technologies, as 
well as related practical achievements, demonstrating the technical pathway of enhancing the efficiency and success rate 
of molecular glue discovery through data-driven and full-process digital management. The symposium further gathered 
global innovation resources, facilitated collaboration between academia and industry, and provided important support for 
the high-quality development of molecular glue drug R&D.
晶泰科技在會上分享了基於AI與自動化技術構建的XGlue™分子膠研發平台及相關實踐成果，展示了以數據驅動和全流
程數字化管理提升分子膠發現效率與成功率的技術路徑。此次研討會進一步匯聚全球創新資源，促進學術界與產業界協
同合作，為分子膠藥物研發邁向高質量發展階段提供了重要支撐。

AI drug R&D technology platform to teachers and students. PatSight, the 
intelligent drug patent analysis tool deployed in the first phase, has been 
launched, and will be expanded to scenarios such as molecular design 
and efficacy evaluation in the future. Integrating artificial intelligence, 
quantum physics computing and cloud computing technologies, the 
platform supports molecular design and virtual screening in large-scale 
chemical space. It not only provides teachers and students with scientific 
research tools including compound design and reaction screening, but 
also cultivates digital R&D talents through industry-academia integration, 
promotes interdisciplinary innovation in pharmacy, chemistry and other 
disciplines, and accelerates the commercialisation of scientific research 
achievements and industrial upgrading in the pharmaceutical field.

Future Scientists Series Activities
未來科學家系列活動
Under XtalPi’s “Future Scientists” study tour brand, the Company partnered with Shenzhen Futian Shixia School to develop 
a special themed study tour curriculum, contributing the innovative strength of a technology enterprise through industry￾university joint training and supporting the digital development of education. The first season of study tour courses 
launched in September 2024. From October to December 2025, three sessions of the second season were successfully 
held in Shenzhen. Following the theoretical thread of the “lock-and-key model”, the programme organised 120 ninth￾grade students to visit the Company and experience the AI-driven drug discovery process in person, building an immersive 
learning scenario from theory to industry. Beyond immersive experience, this season placed greater emphasis on guiding 
students to reflect on the long-term value of the study tour. Through face-to-face exchanges with corporate scientists and 
intuitive exposure to career paths, the programme inspired students to think preliminarily about the relationship between 
personal growth and future social needs, and ignited sustainable interest and internal motivation in these future scientists.
晶泰科技「未來科學家」研學品牌，協同深圳市福田區石廈學校打造專場主題研學課程，以產學聯培方式貢獻科技公司的
創新力量，助力教育數字化發展。自2024年9月開始第一季研學課程，2025年10月至12月期間，第二季活動在深圳成功
舉辦3場。課程延續「鎖鑰模型」理論線索，組織120名九年級學生走進企業，親身體驗AI製藥研發流程，構建從理論到產
業的沉浸式學習場景。本季活動在沉浸式體驗之外，更注重引導學生思考研學體驗的長期價值。通過與企業科學家面對
面交流、對職業路徑的直觀呈現，啟發學生對個人成長與未來社會需求之間關係的初步思考，激發未來科學家們可持續
的興趣與內在動力。

rehabilitation efforts.
2025年11月26日下午，香港大埔宏福苑發生嚴重火災，災情引發社會廣泛關注，各界積極投入救援及重建支持工作。
2025年12月2日，晶泰科技通過香港特區政府設立的「大埔宏福苑援助基金」捐贈150萬港元，用於支持災後救助及善後
工作。

5. 賦能員工 • 同心聚力
公司視人才為企業發展的核心動力，致力於構
建合規、包容、進取的職場環境。我們通過完
善人才引進機制、系統化培養體系和共享型激
勵模式，為員工提供多元發展路徑，並積極營
造安全平等、開放包容的文化氛圍，助力每位
員工實現個人價值。
5.1 引才全球
我們在嚴格遵守合規僱傭要求的基礎上，高度
重視員工權益保障，致力於構建完善的薪酬福
利體系，實現員工與企業的共同成長。
合規僱傭
我們嚴格遵循《中華人民共和國勞動法》《中華
人民共和國勞動合同法》等運營地相關勞動法
規，以《招聘管理制度》規範用工流程，確保招
聘活動合法合規開展。
我們秉持平等僱傭原則，嚴禁僱傭童工及任
何形式的強制勞動。公司的《職場平等與尊重
管理制度》明確禁止在招聘、晉升及日常工作
中出現基於性別、年齡、婚姻狀況、種族、宗
教信仰、殘疾狀況等任何因素的歧視與騷擾行
為。為切實保障員工權益，公司建立了暢通的
溝通渠道和完善的舉報機制，員工可通過實名
或匿名方式對職場不當行為進行舉報，經查證
屬實後將依據制度規定予以嚴肅處理。
多元、平等與包容
我們依託《職場平等與尊重管理制度》為基礎，
配合多維度傳播使包容性價值觀滲透至招聘、
發展、留任等全週期運營環節，以沉浸式文化
活動推動多元理念成為全員共識，最終形成
「和而不同」的全球職場生態，讓每位員工都能
獲得尊重、實現價值、找到歸屬。
5. EMPOWERING EMPLOYEES, UNITING 
STRENGTH
The Company regards talents as the core driving force of enterprise 
development and is committed to building a compliant, inclusive and 
enterprising workplace environment. We provide employees with diversified 
development paths by improving the talent introduction mechanism, the 
systematic training system and the shared incentive model, and actively 
create a safe, equal, open and inclusive cultural atmosphere to help each 
employee realise his/her personal value.
5.1 Introducing Talents from all over the World
We attach great importance to the protection of employees’ rights & 
interests on the basis of strict compliance with compliance employment 
requirements, and are committed to building a sound salary and welfare 
system to realise the common growth of employees and the Company.
Employment compliance
We strictly comply with the Labour Law of the People’s Republic of China
(《中華人民共和國勞動法》), the Labour Contract Law of the People’s 
Republic of China (《中華人民共和國勞動合同法》) and other relevant 
labour laws and regulations of our operating locations, and standardise 
the employee employment process with the Recruitment Management 
System to ensure that the recruitment activities are carried out legally and 
in compliance.
We adhere to the principle of equal employment and strictly prohibit 
the employment of child labour and any form of forced labour. The 
Workplace Equality and Respect Management System of the Company 
explicitly prohibits any discrimination and harassment on the basis of 
any factors such as gender, age, marital status, race, religious belief 
and disability in the process of recruitment, promotion and daily work. 
To effectively protect employees’ rights & interests, the Company has 
established a smooth communication channel and a perfect whistle￾blowing mechanism. Employees can report misconduct in the workplace 
through real name or anonymously, and the Company will seriously deal 
with the relevant employee in accordance with the provisions of systems 
after affirming that such misconduct is true upon verification.
Diversity, equality and inclusion
Based on the Workplace Equality and Respect Management System and 
in conjunction with multi-dimensional dissemination, we have infused the 
inclusive value into the entire operation cycle of recruitment, development 
and retention. Through immersive cultural activities, we have promoted 
the concept of diversity to become a consensus among all employees, 
finally forming a “harmonious but different” global workplace ecology, 
which allows every employee to gain respect, realise his/her value and find 
belonging.

recruitment information and broaden the coverage of social recruitment;

員工福利

人才培養

Employee benefits
We have built a comprehensive employee benefits and care system, 
and are committed to enhancing employees’ sense of happiness and 
belonging. The Company implements a flexible working system and 
flexibly manages the on-duty and off-duty hours to help employees avoid 
peak commuting hours. If an employee is unable to get to the office due 
to special circumstances, the employee can apply for home office on the 
premise of meeting the office conditions and completing the work tasks. 
The Company provides employees with vacation benefits higher than the 
national standard, and adds the annual leave of working years in addition 
to statutory holidays, so as to help employees achieve work-life balance.
On the basis of basic social insurance, we provide employees with 
commercial supplementary insurance and annual comprehensive physical 
examination. The Company also provides employees with transportation, 
meals and nearby rental subsidies to alleviate employees’ living pressure. 
On the statutory holidays and the important moments of employees, 
we convey humanistic care to employees through gifts or souvenirs. 
In addition, we regularly organise team building activities to enrich 
employees’ spare time life and further promote team communication and 
cohesion.

壘；
• 建立跨部門培訓日曆和輕量化學習平台，有效促進跨領域交流與知識整合。

management logic through the Secret to Success for Junior Managers, and provides 
practical documents and the HRBP feedback mechanism, so as to ensure the 
effective improvement of management skills in practice.
• 公司採用訓戰結合模式，通過《初級管理者武林秘籍》解析管理邏輯，並配套實操

人才發展
我們遵循公平公正的晉升原則，以《職級管理
制度》為制度基礎，確保員工晉升流程的規範
性與透明度。公司建立全面的晉升考核體系，
綜合評估員工貢獻度、成長性及影響力等多維
度表現，通過客觀、公正的考評機制衡量員工
表現與發展潛力，為所有員工提供平等的發展
機會與職業成長通道。
我們已建立完善的績效評估體系，通過組織績
效與個人績效雙維度對員工進行全面、系統的
評估。為有效承接公司中長期戰略，推動「戰
略－能力－崗位－人才」體系化對齊，公司於
報告期內正式啟動人才盤點工作，通過構建科
學的人才梯隊畫像，精準識別核心關鍵崗位人
才及高潛力員工，為企業築牢人才長城，支撐
戰略目標實現與可持續發展。
人才保留

化的人才保留舉措，旨在提升核心人才的敬業
度與穩定性，構築堅實的人才壁壘。
Talent development
We follow the principle of fair and just promotion and take the Rank 
Management System as the institutional basis to ensure the standardisation 
and transparency of the employee promotion process. The Company 
has established a comprehensive promotion assessment system to 
comprehensively evaluate the multi-dimensional performance of employees 
such as contribution, growth and influence. The Company measures 
employees’ performance and development potential through an objective 
and fair assessment mechanism, so as to provide all employees with equal 
development opportunities and career growth channels.
We have established a perfect performance evaluation system to 
comprehensively and systematically evaluate employees through the two 
dimensions of organisational performance and personal performance. To 
effectively implement the Company’s medium- and long-term strategy and 
promote the systematic alignment of “strategy-ability-position-talent”, 
the Company officially launched the talent inventory during the Reporting 
Period, and accurately identified the talents in core key positions and high￾potential employees by building a scientific portrait of talent echelon, 
so as to build a great wall of talents for the Company and support the 
realisation of its strategic goals and its sustainable development.
Talent retention
To cope with the increasingly fierce talent competition, we have 
implemented diversified talent retention measures, aiming at improving 
the engagement and stability of core talents and building a solid talent 
barrier.

5.4 Occupational Health and Safety
The Company always puts employees’ occupational health and safety in 
the first place, and continuously optimises the working environment and 
strengthens daily safety propaganda by establishing a sound occupational 
health and safety management system. The Company strictly complies with 
the laws and regulations of our operating locations, and has developed and 
implemented internal policies such as the EHS Management Guidelines and 
the Incident and Accident Management Measures to standardise the safety 
management, so as to fully guarantee the safety of its operation. In the past 
three years, there were no work-related fatalities.
We have set occupational health and safety control objectives, and the 
table below shows the achievement status during the Reporting Period:

To effectively control operational risks, the Company achieved the coverage 
of office space, R&D laboratories and other core areas through the 
combination of internal and external safety inspections, so as to ensure 
timely identification and rectification of potential safety hazards. We 
attach great importance to the prevention and control of occupational 
diseases, and control occupational hazards from the source through multi￾dimensional prevention and control measures: comprehensively carrying out 
occupational health examinations for employees exposed to occupational 
hazards; organising special training based on the production safety month; 
adhere to conducting daily laboratory safety inspections to effectively 
protect employees’ occupational health and the safety of workplaces.
During the Reporting Period, the Company carried out special activities 
such as safety month and comprehensive emergency drills, covering all 
employees of the business department. Through the annual regular drill 
and training, we have effectively improved our employees’ emergency 
response capabilities, reduced the risk of fire accidents, and provided 
solid support for the Company’s compliant operation and sustainable 
development.
During the Reporting Period, we successfully passed the ISO 45001 
Occupational Health and Safety Management System audit and obtained 
certification.

Environmental, Social and Governance Report
6. 綠色發展 • 低碳致遠
面對全球氣候變化挑戰，我們深刻認識到企業
運營對環境的影響與責任。作為一家以科技為
本的企業，我們承諾在追求科學創新與商業成
長的同時，積極以技術推動綠色發展，與各利
益相關方攜手，共同守護生態環境。
6.1 應對氣候變化
氣候變化對人類健康、環境和全球經濟構成威
脅。作為負責任的企業，我們積極識別與評估
氣候變化風險與機遇，以實際行動減少運營碳
足跡，降低氣候變化風險對於企業運營及研發
的潛在影響。為此，我們構建氣候變化管理體
系，參考氣候相關財務信息披露工作組(TCFD)
的建議框架，從治理、戰略、風險管理及指標
與目標四個維度，全面提升氣候信息披露的透
明性。
6. GREEN DEVELOPMENT • LOW CARBON PRIORITY
Facing the challenge brought by global climate change, we deeply 
realise the impact and responsibility of an enterprise’s operation on the 
environment. As a technology-based enterprise, we promise to actively 
promote green development with technology while pursuing scientific 
innovation and business growth, and work together with all stakeholders 
to jointly protect the ecological environment.
6.1 Addressing Climate Change
Climate change poses a threat to the human health, the environment and 
the global economy. As a responsible enterprise, we actively identify and 
evaluate the risks and opportunities of climate change, and take practical 
actions to reduce the carbon footprint of operations and reduce the 
potential impact of climate change risks on enterprises’ operations and 
R&D. To this end, we have built a climate change management system, 
and comprehensively improved the transparency of climate information 
disclosure from four dimensions of governance, strategy, risk management 
and indicators and targets with reference to the suggested framework of 
the Task Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosures (TCFD).

[PDF PAGE 176]
年度報告 2025 晶泰控股有限公司 175
環境、社會及管治報告
Environmental, Social and Governance Report
Governance
We have established a climate change governance structure consisting 
of the Board of Directors, the ESG Working Group and the climate 
change-related business and functional departments. Relying on a sound 
governance system, we systematically identify, evaluate and address 
climate change risks and opportunities, and have gradually established a 
systematic climate response and normalised management mechanism by 
implementing management measures such as energy conservation and 
emission reduction and green operation.
管治
我們搭建了由董事會、ESG工作組、氣候變化
相關業務及職能部門組成的氣候變化治理架
構。依託完善的治理體系，系統識別、評估並
應對氣候變化風險與機遇，通過落實節能減排
與綠色運營等管理措施，逐步建立系統性的氣
候應對與常態化管理機制。
我們在ESG培訓中為董事會成員提供了氣候變
化合規披露趨勢相關內容，幫助他們獲取必要
的專業知識，以勝任對氣候變化相關問題的管
理和監督。
In the ESG training, we provided the members of the Board of Directors 
with the contents related to the trend of climate change compliance 
disclosure to help them acquire the necessary professional knowledge 
to be competent in the management and supervision of climate change￾related issues.
Supervision by the Board of Directors
董事會監督
• The Board of Directors formulates strategies to deal with risks and opportunities 
related to climate change
董事會制定應對氣候變化相關風險與機遇的策略
• Under the supervision by the Board of Directors, the Company continuously identies 
and updates the potential risks and opportunities brought by climate change, and 
evaluates the direct and indirect impact of climate change on the Company’s business.
在董事會監督下，公司持續識別並更新氣候變化帶來的潛在風險與機遇，評估其對業務的直
接與間接影響。
Guidance by the ESG Working Group
ESG工作組指導
• The ESG Working Group coordinates and guides the identication and assessment of 
climate change risks, organises various functional departments to formulate coping 
strategies, and regularly reviews the overall progress.
ESG工作組統籌指導氣候變化風險識別與評估工作，組織各職能部門制定應對策略，並定期
回顧整體進展。
Implementation by relevant business and functional departments
相關業務及職能部門執行
• All functional departments are responsible for implementing specic 
countermeasures, actively promoting energy conservation and emission reduction 
and green operation, continuously reducing carbon emissions and improving the 
energy use efciency.
各職能部門負責落實具體應對措施，並積極推動節能減排與綠色運營，持續降低碳排放、
提升能源使用效率。
